### `components/appbar/`

#### `<md-gb-app-bar>`

A Material Design app bar component.

- Class: `AppBarElement` in `components/appbar/app-bar-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/appbar/md-gb-app-bar.js';`
- Spec: [app-bars](https://m3.material.io/components/app-bars)
- Spec sections cited: `app-bars/specs/anatomy`, `app-bars/guidelines/anatomy`, `app-bars/guidelines/anatomy/headline`, `app-bars/guidelines/anatomy/leading-button`, `app-bars/specs/anatomy/subtitle`, `app-bars/specs/anatomy/search`, `app-bars/guidelines/usage`, `app-bars/specs/variants`, `app-bars/specs/baseline-app-bars`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `centered` | `centered` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the title and subtitle are centered instead of leading-aligned (the M3 Expressive "centered text" configuration). Defaults to `false`. |
| `scrollBehavior` | `scroll-behavior` (reflects) | `"hide" \| "compress"` |   | How the app bar reacts to scrolling: `hide` (hide on scroll down, reveal on scroll up) or `compress` (medium and large flexible app bars become small until scrolled back to the top). |
| `scrolled` | `scrolled` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the app bar is in its scrolled state. Defaults to `false`. A medium or large flexible app bar in its scrolled state is shown as a small one (app-bars.md: "When scrolled, medium flexible and large flexible app bars can transform into small app bars"). |
| `scrollTarget` | `scroll-target` | `string` | `''` | The id of the element whose scrolling drives `scroll-behavior`. Defaults to the window. |
| `size` | `size` (reflects) | `"sm" \| "md" \| "lg"` | `'sm'` | The size of the app bar (`sm`, `md`, `lg`). Defaults to `sm`. |
| `variant` | `variant` (reflects) | `"standard" \| "search"` | `'standard'` | The behavioral variant of the app bar (`standard` or `search`). Defaults to `standard`. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `overflowchange` | `CustomEvent<{overflowed: Element[]}>` | Fired when the trailing actions that collapse at the current window size class change. --bubbles --composed |
| `scrollstatechange` | `CustomEvent<{scrolled: boolean}>` | Fired when the scrolled state changes. --bubbles --composed |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Used to display the primary title text of the app bar. |
| `image` | An optional image or logo. In a small app bar it can replace the title text; in medium and large flexible app bars it appears above the text. |
| `leading` | Used to display the leading navigation icon button (e.g. menu or back). |
| `overflow` | Used to display an overflow menu button. Shown only while trailing actions are collapsed (see "Adaptive design" above). |
| `search` | Used to display the search bar input or controls when `variant="search"`, inside the search bar container. |
| `subtitle` | Used to display an optional subtitle below the title. |
| `tabs` | Optional `md-gb-tabs` attached below the app bar. They are part of the app bar's box, so they hide and reveal with it as one unit (tabs.md: "When tabs are attached to a component, they should appear and move as a single unit"). |
| `trailing` | Used to display trailing action buttons and/or user avatar. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `app-bar` | The root container element. |

- Spec sections cited: `all-buttons/tab-1/there-are-10-types-of-buttons-in-material-3`, `all-buttons/tab-1/choosing-buttons`, `buttons/specs/anatomy`, `buttons/guidelines/anatomy`, `buttons/guidelines/usage`, `buttons/specs/variants`, `buttons/specs/configurations`, `buttons/specs/color`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `color` | `color` | `"filled" \| "elevated" \| "tonal" \| "outlined" \| "text"` | `'text'` | The color of the button. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | `boolean` |   | Whether or not the element is disabled. |
| `download` | `download` | `string` | `''` | The filename to use when downloading the linked resource. If not specified, the browser will determine a filename. This is only applicable when the button is used as a link (`href` is set). |
| `href` | `href` | `string` | `''` | The URL that the link button points to. |
| `name` | `name` | `string` |   | The HTML name to use in form submission. When combined with a `value`, the submitting button's name/value will be added to the form. Names must reflect to a `name` attribute for form integration. The HTML name to use in form submission. |
| `referrerPolicy` | `referrerpolicy` | `string` | `''` | Sets the underlying `HTMLAnchorElement`'s `referrerpolicy` attribute when `href` is set. |
| `rel` | `rel` | `string` | `''` | Sets the underlying `HTMLAnchorElement`'s `rel` attribute when `href` is set. |
| `selected` | `selected` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether or not the button is selected, when `type="toggle"`. TODO: replace reflected `[selected]` with `:state(selected)` |
| `size` | `size` | `"sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xs" \| "xl"` | `'sm'` | The size of the button. |
| `softDisabled` | `soft-disabled` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether or not the button is "soft-disabled" (disabled but still focusable). Use this when a button needs increased visibility when disabled. See https://www.w3.org/WAI/ARIA/apg/practices/keyboard-interface/#kbd_disabled_controls for more guidance on when this is needed. |
| `square` | `square` | `boolean` | `false` | Changes the shape of the button to be square. |
| `target` | `target` | `"" \| "_blank" \| "_parent" \| "_self" \| "_top"` | `''` | Where to display the linked `href` URL for a link button. Common options include `_blank` to open in a new tab. |
| `type` | `type` (reflects) | `string` |   | A string indicating the behavior of the button. - "submit" (default): A button that submits its associated form. - "reset": A button that resets its associated form. - "button": A normal button. - "toggle": A toggle button using the `selected` property. - "link": An anchor link (`<a>`). Type is always "link" when `href` is set. |
| `unfilledIcon` | `unfilled-icon` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Set when the icon has no filled version: a selected toggle button then shows the icon in semibold weight instead of filled. |
| `value` | `value` (reflects) | `string` |   | The value of the button. When combined with a `name`, the submitting button's name/value will be added to the form. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `change` | `Event` | Fired when a toggle button is selected or unselected. --bubbles |

### `components/buttongroup/`

#### `<md-gb-button-group>`

A Material Design button group.

- Class: `ButtonGroupElement` in `components/buttongroup/button-group-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/buttongroup/md-gb-button-group.js';`
- Spec: [button-groups](https://m3.material.io/components/button-groups)
- Spec sections cited: `button-groups/specs/variants`, `button-groups/specs/states/connected-button-group`, `button-groups/specs/configurations`, `button-groups/specs/anatomy/color`, `button-groups/specs/states/standard-button-group`, `button-groups/specs/anatomy/common-layouts`, `button-groups/specs/anatomy`, `button-groups/guidelines/anatomy`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density of the buttons inside, from 0 (default) to -3. Each step down removes 4px from the buttons' height (button-groups.md: "Button groups adapt to the height of the buttons inside, including when density is applied"); touch targets stay 48px. A button's own non-zero `density` wins. Values are clamped to that range. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the button group is disabled. |
| `overflowMenu` | `overflow-menu` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the buttons at the trailing edge that do not fit collapse into a trailing overflow menu, and come back when the group has room again (button-groups.md, Adaptive design: "Buttons at the trailing edge of the button group can be customized to collapse into an overflow menu at smaller breakpoints, and become visible again at larger sizes."). The group is as wide as its container allows: give it a width, or put it in a narrower container, to make buttons overflow. Choosing a menu entry clicks the original button. |
| `required` | `required` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether selection is required in single-select mode. |
| `resizing` | `resizing` (reflects) | `"" \| "fixed" \| "flexible"` | `""` | How the group and its buttons resize (button-groups.md, Resizing). `fixed`: every button keeps the width its own size and width give it. `flexible`: the group fills the width of its container and its buttons grow with it; an icon button does not stretch past its set width, and a button with `resizing="fixed"` keeps its width. A button with `resizing="flexible"` in a fixed group grows when the group is given more width. Unset: a connected group is flexible (it spans its surface), a standard group is fixed. |
| `selection` | `selection` (reflects) | `"none" \| "single" \| "multiple"` | `"none"` | The selection mode. |
| `variant` | `variant` (reflects) | `"standard" \| "connected"` | `"standard"` | The visual variant of the button group. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `change` | `Event` | Fired when selection changes. --bubbles |
| `input` | `InputEvent` | Fired when selection changes. --bubbles --composed |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Used to display buttons. |
| `overflow` | Replaces the default overflow button (see `overflowMenu`). |

### `components/card/`

#### `<md-gb-card>`

A Material Design component.

- Class: `CardElement` in `components/card/card-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/card/md-gb-card.js';`
- Spec: [cards](https://m3.material.io/components/cards)
- Spec sections cited: `cards/specs/elevated-card`, `cards/specs/filled-card`, `cards/specs/outlined-card`, `cards/guidelines/usage`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `color` | `color` | `"filled" \| "elevated" \| "outlined"` | `'outlined'` | The color of the card. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the card is disabled. |
| `dragged` | `dragged` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the card is picked up and being dragged (raised elevation). The app moves the card. Spec Cards > Accessibility > Dragging and dismissing (WCAG 2.5.7): also offer a single-pointer alternative, e.g. a menu opened by tapping a "more" button or pressing and holding the card, with move and delete actions (see the "Reorder (drag or menu)" demo). Don't let the menu cover the card. |
| `href` | `href` | `string` | `''` | Makes an interactive card a link (spec: directly actionable cards have the button or link role). |
| `interactive` | `interactive` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the card is interactive. |
| `selectable` | `selectable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether an interactive card can be selected: activating it toggles `selected` (selection.md lists cards among the components that can be selected). |
| `selected` | `selected` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether a `selectable` card is selected. A selected card shows a check mark and a change in surface color. |
| `target` | `target` | `"" \| "_blank" \| "_parent" \| "_self" \| "_top"` | `''` | The link target, when `href` is set. |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Used to display the card's content. The card has 16dp left/right padding; set `--padding-inline: 0` for full-bleed content. Add padding to content, not the host <md-gb-card> element. |
| `container` | Used to set a custom background container for the card. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `card` | The card's root element. |
| `card-btn` | The card's main action (a button, or a link when `href` is set), when interactive. |

| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density, from 0 (default) to -3. Each step down removes 4px from the checkbox's layout box (48px) and state layer (40px); the touch target stays 48px. Values are clamped to that range. checkbox.md: don't apply density by default; let people opt in to a denser layout. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | `boolean` |   | Disabled by the attribute or by a disabled fieldset. |
| `error` | `error` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether or not the checkbox is invalid. |
| `indeterminate` | `indeterminate` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether or not the checkbox is indeterminate. https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/input/checkbox#indeterminate_state_checkboxes |
| `name` | `name` | `string` |   | The HTML name to use in form submission. |
| `required` | `required` | `boolean` | `false` | When true, require the checkbox to be selected when participating in form submission. https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/input/checkbox#validation |
| `value` | `value` | `string` | `'on'` | The value of the checkbox that is submitted with a form when selected. https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/input/checkbox#value |

| `formResetCallback` | `() => void` | An optional callback for when the form requests to reset its value. Typically, the default value that is reset is represented in the attribute of an element. This means the attribute used for the value should not update as the value changes. For example, a checkbox should not change its default `checked` attribute when selected. Ensure form values do not reflect. |
| `formStateRestoreCallback` | `(state: string) => void` | An optional callback for when the form restores the state of a component. For example, when a page is reloaded or forms are autofilled. |

| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density, from 0 (default) to -3. Each step down removes 4px from the container height (32px); the label and icons keep their size and the touch target stays 48px (chips.md: "regardless of placement or density"). Values are clamped to that range. Density 0 inherits the density of an enclosing `md-gb-chip-set`. chips.md: don't apply density by default; let people opt in to a denser layout. |

| `dragged` | `dragged` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the chip is being dragged (spec dragged state elevation). |
| `editable` | `editable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the text of an input chip can be edited (chips.md "Input chips": "Input chips can support editing to change their contents, such as correcting an email address"). Editing starts by interacting with the chip: a second click, Enter or Space on the selected chip, or a double click. Committing with Enter or by leaving the field changes the chip's text and fires `edit`; Escape (or emptying the text) keeps the old text. |
| `elevated` | `elevated` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Elevated container, for chips on images or complex backgrounds. Input chips have no elevated style. |
| `groupDisabled` |   | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the chip's set is disabled (a disabled form fieldset). |
| `href` | `href` | `string` | `""` | The URL destination; makes an assist chip a link. |
| `referrerPolicy` | `referrerpolicy` | `string` | `""` | Sets the underlying `HTMLAnchorElement`'s `referrerpolicy` attribute when `href` is set. |
| `rel` | `rel` | `string` | `""` | Sets the underlying `HTMLAnchorElement`'s `rel` attribute when `href` is set. |
| `removable` | `removable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the chip has a remove button (input chips). |
| `removeLabel` | `remove-label` | `string` | `""` | The remove button's accessible label. Defaults to "Remove {chip label}" (spec: the remove action is labeled separately). |
| `selected` | `selected` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the filter (or input) chip is selected. |
| `softDisabled` | `soft-disabled` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the chip is soft-disabled (`aria-disabled="true"` while preserving focusability). |
| `target` | `target` | `"" \| "_blank" \| "_parent" \| "_self" \| "_top"` | `""` | Where to display the linked `href` URL for a link chip. |
| `type` | `type` | `"assist" \| "filter" \| "input" \| "suggestion" \| "action" \| "toggle" \| "link"` | `"assist"` | The chip type: `assist` (default), `filter`, `input` or `suggestion`. |
| `value` | `value` | `string` |   | The value a chip set submits while the chip is selected. Defaults to the label text, like a native `<option>`. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `cancelEdit` | `() => void` | Ends editing and keeps the old text. |
| `commitEdit` | `() => void` | Ends editing and applies the new text (empty or unchanged text keeps the old). |
| `focus` | `(options?: FocusOptions & { trailing?: boolean; }) => void` | Focuses the chip's primary action, or its remove button with `{trailing: true}` (chip set navigation backwards into a chip). |
| `startEdit` | `() => void` | Turns an editable input chip into a text string to edit. Does nothing for other chips. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `change` | `Event` | Fired when a filter chip's selection changes. --bubbles |
| `edit` | `CustomEvent` | Fired when an `editable` chip's new text is committed; `detail` has `value` and `previousValue`. --bubbles --composed |
| `input` | `InputEvent` | Fired when a filter chip's selection changes. --bubbles --composed |
| `remove` | `Event` | Fired when the remove button is activated, or Backspace / Delete is pressed on a removable chip. --bubbles --composed |
| `update-focus` | `Event` | Fired when `disabled` changes, so a chip set can update its tab stop. --bubbles |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | The chip label text. |
| `icon` | An optional leading icon, or an avatar (`class="chip-avatar"` or an `<img>`; input chips). |
| `remove-icon` | The remove button icon when `removable` (default: the `close` symbol). |
| `trailing-icon` | An optional trailing icon (filter chips, e.g. a drop-down arrow). |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `chip` | The root container element. |

#### `<md-gb-chip-set>`

A Material Design Expressive chip set.

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density of the chips, from 0 (default) to -3: each step down removes 4px from the chips' height. A chip's own non-zero `density` wins. Values are clamped to that range. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | `boolean` |   | Disabled by the attribute or by a disabled fieldset. |
| `name` | `name` | `string` |   | The HTML name to use in form submission. |
| `required` | `required` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Single-select: the selected chip can't be unselected. |
| `selection` | `selection` (reflects) | `"none" \| "single" \| "multiple"` | `"multiple"` | How many filter chips can be selected: `none`, `single` or `multiple`. |
| `singleLine` | `single-line` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Keep the chips on one line and scroll horizontally instead of wrapping. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `formDisabledCallback` | `(disabled: boolean) => void` | A callback for when a form component should be disabled or enabled. This can be called in a variety of situations, such as disabled `<fieldset>`s. |
| `formResetCallback` | `() => void` | An optional callback for when the form requests to reset its value. Typically, the default value that is reset is represented in the attribute of an element. This means the attribute used for the value should not update as the value changes. For example, a checkbox should not change its default `checked` attribute when selected. Ensure form values do not reflect. |
| `formStateRestoreCallback` | `(state: FormRestoreState) => void` | An optional callback for when the form restores the state of a component. For example, when a page is reloaded or forms are autofilled. |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | The chips. |

### `components/datepicker/`

#### `<md-gb-date-picker>`

A Material Design date picker (docked or modal, single date or range).

- Class: `DatePickerElement` in `components/datepicker/date-picker-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/datepicker/md-gb-date-picker.js';`
- Spec: [date-pickers](https://m3.material.io/components/date-pickers)
- Spec sections cited: `date-pickers/specs/modal-date-picker`, `date-pickers/guidelines/anatomy/full-screen-date-picker`, `date-pickers/guidelines/anatomy/docked-date-picker`, `date-pickers/specs/modal-date-picker/modal-date-picker-configurations`, `date-pickers/specs/docked-date-picker`, `date-pickers/specs/modal-date-input/modal-date-input-configurations`, `date-pickers/guidelines/anatomy/modal-date-picker`, `date-pickers/guidelines/anatomy/modal-date-input`, `date-pickers/guidelines/usage`, `date-pickers/specs/modal-date-input`, `date-pickers/specs/docked-date-picker/docked-date-picker-configurations`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `clearable` | `clearable` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows a Clear button that removes the selection. |
| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density of the picker's text fields (the docked field and the modal input mode's fields), from 0 (default) to -3, as `md-gb-text-field` `density`. Values are clamped to that range. The calendar itself never gets denser (date-pickers.md, Accessibility: "Interactive targets for all elements meet Material's 48x48dp minimum touch target requirement. Increasing density would negatively impact accessibility by limiting tappable/clickable targets"): its dates, menus and buttons keep their size, including inside a dense container. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | `boolean` |   | Disabled by the attribute or by a disabled fieldset. |
| `error` | `error` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Forces the error state of the docked field. |
| `errorText` | `error-text` | `string` | `''` | The message shown on the docked field while `error` is set. |
| `firstDayOfWeek` | `first-day-of-week` | `number` | `-1` | The first day of the week, 0 (Sunday) to 6 (Saturday). -1 (default) uses the locale's. |
| `fullscreen` | `fullscreen` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Modal: the full-screen layout the spec recommends on compact windows. The dialog covers the window and has a close (x) icon button and a **Save** button in place of Cancel and OK. |
| `hideActions` | `hide-actions` | `boolean` | `false` | Hides the Cancel and OK buttons; a selection is committed right away. |
| `inline` | `inline` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Renders the calendar surface in the page flow, always visible, instead of a docked popup (without the text field) or a modal dialog. |
| `isDateDisabled` |   | `(date: string) => boolean` | `null` | Returns true for dates that can't be selected (in addition to dates outside `min` / `max`). Receives an ISO date. |
| `label` | `label` | `string` | `''` | Docked: the text field label. Defaults to "Date" (range: "Dates"). |
| `locale` | `locale` | `string` | `''` | A BCP 47 locale. Defaults to the document's `lang`, else the browser's. |
| `max` | `max` | `string` | `''` | The latest selectable date (ISO). |
| `min` | `min` | `string` | `''` | The earliest selectable date (ISO). |
| `mode` | `mode` (reflects) | `"input" \| "picker"` | `'picker'` | Modal: shows the calendar (`picker`) or the date input (`input`). |
| `name` | `name` | `string` |   | The HTML name to use in form submission. |
| `open` | `open` (reflects) | `boolean` |   | Whether the popup (docked) or dialog (modal) is open. |
| `quick` | `quick` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Skips animations. |
| `range` | `range` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Selects a start and end date. |
| `required` | `required` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | A value is required: with no date (or a range without an end date) the picker is invalid (`valueMissing`). |
| `strings` |   | `Partial<DatePickerStrings>` | `{}` | Overrides for the user-facing strings. |
| `supportingText` | `supporting-text` | `string` | `''` | Docked: the field's supporting text. Defaults to the date format, e.g. MM/DD/YYYY. |
| `value` | `value` | `string` | `''` | The committed value: an ISO date (`YYYY-MM-DD`), or an ISO interval (`start/end`) when `range` is set. Empty when nothing is selected. |
| `variant` | `variant` (reflects) | `"docked" \| "modal"` | `'docked'` | `docked` (default) or `modal`. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `cancel` | `(reason?: string) => boolean` | Discards the pending selection: fires a cancelable `cancel` event, then closes the popup or dialog. |
| `close` | `(reason?: string) => Promise<void>` | Closes the popup or dialog without changing the value (use `confirm()` to commit the pending selection). |
| `confirm` | `() => boolean` | Commits the pending selection to `value` (firing `change` if it changed) and closes the popup or dialog. In the date input mode, invalid text shows its error and keeps the picker open. |
| `focus` | `(options?: FocusOptions) => void` | [MDN Reference](https://developer.mozilla.org/docs/Web/API/HTMLElement/focus) |
| `formDisabledCallback` | `(disabled: boolean) => void` | A callback for when a form component should be disabled or enabled. This can be called in a variety of situations, such as disabled `<fieldset>`s. |
| `formResetCallback` | `() => void` | An optional callback for when the form requests to reset its value. Typically, the default value that is reset is represented in the attribute of an element. This means the attribute used for the value should not update as the value changes. For example, a checkbox should not change its default `checked` attribute when selected. Ensure form values do not reflect. |
| `formStateRestoreCallback` | `(state: string) => void` | An optional callback for when the form restores the state of a component. For example, when a page is reloaded or forms are autofilled. |
| `show` | `() => Promise<void>` | Opens the calendar popup (docked) or dialog (modal). The pending selection starts from `value`, and focus moves to the selected date (or today). |
| `showMonth` | `(year: number, month: number) => void` | Shows the given month (`year`, `month` 1-12) in the calendar. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `cancel` | `Event` | The selection was dismissed (Cancel, Escape or a click outside). Cancelable: canceling keeps the picker open. |
| `change` | `CustomEvent<DatePickerValueDetail>` | The value was committed (OK, Enter, typing in the docked field, or a pick with `hide-actions`). --bubbles |
| `close` | `CustomEvent<{reason: string}>` | The popup or dialog began closing; `reason` is `confirm` or `cancel`. |
| `input` | `CustomEvent<DatePickerValueDetail>` | The pending selection changed (a date was picked or a valid date typed). --bubbles --composed |
| `open` | `Event` | The popup or dialog opened. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `actions` | The text buttons row. |
| `container` | The picker surface. |
| `field` | The docked text field. |
| `grid` | The month grid. |
| `header` | The header (modal headline region, or docked menus). |

### `components/dialog/`

#### `<md-gb-dialog>`

A Material Design dialog component.

- Class: `DialogElement` in `components/dialog/dialog-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/dialog/md-gb-dialog.js';`
- Spec: [dialogs](https://m3.material.io/components/dialogs)
- Spec sections cited: `dialogs/specs/full-screen-dialogs`, `dialogs/guidelines/anatomy/full-screen-dialog`, `dialogs/specs/basic-dialogs/basic-dialog-measurements`, `dialogs/specs/basic-dialogs`, `dialogs/guidelines/anatomy/basic-dialog`, `dialogs/guidelines/usage`, `dialogs/guidelines/adaptive-design`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `alwaysFullScreen` | `always-full-screen` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Full-screen dialogs are for compact windows only (dialogs.md: use a basic dialog at medium and expanded). So `full-screen` applies at compact windows and the dialog is a basic dialog above, with the same slots. The host gets `adaptive-basic` while it falls back. Set `always-full-screen` to keep the full-screen variant at every window size. |
| `closeLabel` | `close-label` | `string` | `'Close'` | Accessible label of the full-screen close icon button. |
| `fullScreen` | `full-screen` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Uses the full-screen variant: the dialog fills the window, with a header holding a close icon button, the headline and the confirm action. Meant for compact windows; use a basic dialog at larger breakpoints. |
| `noFocusTrap` | `no-focus-trap` | `boolean` | `false` | Turns off focus trapping, which keeps Tab and Shift+Tab cycling inside the dialog. Trapping is recommended for accessibility. |
| `open` | `open` (reflects) | `boolean` |   | Opens the dialog when set to `true` and closes it when set to `false`. |
| `quick` | `quick` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Skips the opening and closing animations. |
| `returnValue` |   | `string` | `''` | The dialog's return value, usually which button closed it. Set by `close(returnValue)` and by `<form method="dialog">` submitters' `value`. Canceling (Escape, scrim, close button) leaves it unchanged. https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLDialogElement/returnValue |
| `type` | `type` | `"" \| "alert" \| "dialog"` | `''` | The dialog's accessibility role. `alert` (`role="alertdialog"`) or `dialog` (`role="dialog"`). When empty, basic dialogs are alert dialogs, as the spec asks for on the web, and full-screen dialogs are dialogs. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `(returnValue?: string, reason?: string) => Promise<void>` | Closes the dialog and fires a cancelable `close` event, then `closed` after the exit animation. Focus returns to the element focused before the dialog opened. |
| `show` | `() => Promise<void>` | Opens the dialog as a modal and fires a cancelable `open` event, then `opened` after the enter animation. Focus moves to the first child with an `autofocus` attribute, else the first focusable element, else the dialog. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `cancel` | `Event` | Fired when the dialog is canceled with Escape, a scrim click or the full-screen close button. Cancelable; canceling keeps the dialog open. |
| `close` | `Event` | Fired when the dialog begins closing; `returnValue` is already set. Cancelable. |
| `closed` | `Event` | Fired when the dialog has closed and animations finished. |
| `open` | `Event` | Fired when the dialog begins opening. Cancelable. |
| `opened` | `Event` | Fired when the dialog has opened and animations finished. |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | The supporting text or custom content (lists, form fields). Scrolls between the pinned headline and actions when it overflows. |
| `actions` | The action buttons, dismissive first and confirming last. Basic: end-aligned below the content. Full-screen: the confirm action at the end of the header. |
| `headline` | The headline. Labels the dialog (`aria-labelledby`). |
| `icon` | An optional hero icon above the headline (basic only). Centers the headline. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `actions` | The actions container. |
| `close-button` | The full-screen close icon button. |
| `content` | The content container. |
| `header` | The headline region (full-screen: the header bar). |
| `headline` | The headline element. |
| `icon` | The hero icon container. |
| `scroller` | The scroll container of the content. |

| `setupDialog` | function | `(dialog: HTMLElement, opts?: { signal?: AbortSignal; }): void` | Sets up Tab focus trapping on a modal `<dialog>`: Tab on the last focusable element wraps to the first, and Shift+Tab on the first wraps to the last. A native modal dialog makes the rest of the page inert, but lets Tab leave the page for the browser UI; this keeps focus cycling inside the dialog. Add `data-no-focus-trap` to the dialog to turn it off. |

| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density, from 0 (default) to -3. Each step down removes 4px from the container height (and the width of a default-width button); the icon keeps its size and the touch target stays at least 48px. Values are clamped to that range. Density 0 inherits the density of an enclosing `md-gb-button-group`. icon-buttons.md: don't apply density by default; let people opt in to a denser layout. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | `boolean` |   | Whether or not the element is disabled. |
| `download` | `download` | `string` | `''` | The filename to use when downloading the linked resource. |
| `href` | `href` | `string` | `''` | The URL that the link button points to. |
| `name` | `name` | `string` |   | The HTML name to use in form submission. When combined with a `value`, the submitting button's name/value will be added to the form. Names must reflect to a `name` attribute for form integration. The HTML name to use in form submission. |
| `referrerPolicy` | `referrerpolicy` | `string` | `''` | Sets the underlying `HTMLAnchorElement`'s `referrerpolicy` attribute when `href` is set. |
| `rel` | `rel` | `string` | `''` | Sets the underlying `HTMLAnchorElement`'s `rel` attribute when `href` is set. |
| `selected` | `selected` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether or not the button is selected, when `type="toggle"`. TODO: replace reflected `[selected]` with `:state(selected)` |
| `size` | `size` | `"sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xs" \| "xl"` | `'sm'` | The size of the button. |
| `softDisabled` | `soft-disabled` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether or not the button is "soft-disabled" (disabled but still focusable). |
| `square` | `square` | `boolean` | `false` | Changes the shape of the button to be square. |
| `target` | `target` | `"" \| "_blank" \| "_parent" \| "_self" \| "_top"` | `''` | Where to display the linked `href` URL for a link button. |
| `tooltip` | `tooltip` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Shows a tooltip with the button's accessibility label (`aria-label`) on hover and keyboard focus. The label should describe the action, not name the icon. |
| `type` | `type` (reflects) | `string` |   | A string indicating the behavior of the button. - "submit" (default): A button that submits its associated form. - "reset": A button that resets its associated form. - "button": A normal button. - "toggle": A toggle button using the `selected` property. - "link": An anchor link (`<a>`). Type is always "link" when `href` is set. |
| `unfilledIcon` | `unfilled-icon` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Set when the icon has no filled version: a selected toggle button then shows the icon in semibold weight instead of filled. |
| `value` | `value` (reflects) | `string` |   | The value of the button. When combined with a `name`, the submitting button's name/value will be added to the form. |
| `width` | `width` | `"" \| "narrow" \| "wide"` | `''` | Changes the width of the button. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `change` | `Event` | Fired when a toggle button is selected or unselected. --bubbles |
| `input` | `InputEvent` | Fired when a toggle icon button is selected or unselected. --bubbles --composed |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Used to display an icon. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `icon-btn` | The icon button's root element. |

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density, from 0 (default) to -3. Each step down removes 4px from the items' height (spec foundations Density: "decreasing the top and bottom padding or overall height by 4dp"). Values are clamped to that range. Like `md-gb-segmented-button-set`'s `density`. |
| `reorderable` | `reorderable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the items can be reordered. Shows a drag handle on every item. |
| `reorderMessages` |   | `ListReorderMessages` | `LIST_REORDER_MESSAGES` | The screen reader announcements made while reordering. |
| `segmented` | `segmented` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether to render the list with segmented items. |
| `selectable` | `selectable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the user can select several items with a long press (and drag), and by tapping them while in selection mode. |
| `selectionPrimary` | `selection-primary` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether selecting is the list's primary activity: a `selectable` list then always shows the selection checkboxes (on desktop). |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `itemselectionchange` | `Event` | When the user changes the selected items of a `selectable` list (`ListSelectionChangeEvent` with `selected`). |
| `reorder` | `Event` | When the user drops an item at a new position (`ListReorderEvent` with `from`, `to` and `item`). Cancelable. |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Used to display list items. |

| --- | --- |

#### `<md-gb-list-item>`

A Material Design component.

- Class: `ListItemElement` in `components/list/list-item-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/list/md-gb-list-item.js';`
- Spec: [lists](https://m3.material.io/components/lists)
- Spec sections cited: `lists/guidelines/anatomy/selection-controls`, `lists/guidelines/anatomy`, `lists/specs/anatomy`, `lists/specs/anatomy/flexibility-slots/slot-accessibility`, `lists/guidelines/anatomy/leading-media/primary-secondary-actions`, `lists/specs/configurations/list-interactions`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `checked` | `checked` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the list item is selected. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the list item is disabled. |
| `dragging` | `dragging` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the item is being dragged. Set by the list while reordering. |
| `dragHandleLabel` | `drag-handle-label` | `string` | `"Reorder"` | The accessible label prefix of the drag handle; the item's label text is appended (e.g. "Reorder Apples"). |
| `expandable` | `expandable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the item expands to reveal the content in its `nested` slot. Expandable items are always interactive. |
| `expanded` | `expanded` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether an `expandable` item is expanded. |
| `grabbed` | `grabbed` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the item is grabbed for reordering without dragging (with the keyboard or a tap on the drag handle). Set by the list; shows the move up and move down buttons. |
| `href` | `href` (reflects) | `string` | `""` | Makes the item a link to this URL: a single-action item whose action is navigating (lists.md, Single-action: "the entire list item performs one action, such as navigating to a new page"). Not for an expandable, selectable or disabled item, which stays a button. |
| `moveDownLabel` | `move-down-label` | `string` | `"Move down"` | The accessible label prefix of the move down button; the item's label text is appended. |
| `moveUpLabel` | `move-up-label` | `string` | `"Move up"` | The accessible label prefix of the move up button; the item's label text is appended (e.g. "Move up Apples"). |
| `nonInteractive` | `static` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the list item is non-interactive. |
| `noRevealButton` | `no-reveal-button` | `boolean` | `false` | Hides the "more" button that reveals the swipe actions with a tap. Only set it when the app offers another single-pointer way to reach them (e.g. a menu), as the spec requires one. |
| `reorderable` | `reorderable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether to show a drag handle to reorder the item within its `md-gb-list`. Set by the list when the list is `reorderable`. |
| `revealButtonLabel` | `reveal-button-label` | `string` | `"More actions"` | The accessible label prefix of the "more" button; the item's label text is appended (e.g. "More actions Apples"). |
| `revealed` | `revealed` (reflects) | `"" \| "leading" \| "trailing"` | `""` | The side whose swipe actions are revealed, or `''` when closed. |
| `rovingTabIndex` |   | `number` | `null` | Set by a parent `md-gb-list` to make the list a single tab stop with arrow-key navigation (roving tabindex, lists.md Keyboard navigation): `0` on the list's tab stop, `-1` on the other items, `null` (default) for an item outside a list. While set, the item's other controls (drag handle, "more" and move buttons, actions) leave the tab order and are reached with Left/Right. |
| `selectable` |   | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the item shows a selection checkbox (hover, or whenever `selectionVisible`). Set by a `selectable` parent `md-gb-list`. |
| `selectionVisible` | `selection-visible` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the selection checkbox is always shown: the list sets it when selection is the primary activity or once an item is selected (selection.md, "Click"). Otherwise the checkbox shows on hover. |
| `target` | `target` | `"" \| "_blank" \| "_parent" \| "_self" \| "_top"` | `""` | The link target, when `href` is set. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `() => void` | Hides the swipe actions. |
| `focusDragHandle` | `() => void` | Focuses the item's drag handle, if it has one. |
| `focusPrimary` | `() => void` | Focuses the item itself (its primary action), or its first control when the item is `static` or disabled. Used by `md-gb-list`'s arrow keys. |
| `reveal` | `(side: "leading" \| "trailing") => void` | Reveals the swipe actions on one side. |
| `rowElements` | `() => HTMLElement[]` | The item's focusable elements in visual order (start to end): leading actions, interactive `avatar`/`leading` content, the item, interactive `trailing` content, the "more" button, the move up / move down buttons, the drag handle and the trailing actions. Left/Right move through them. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `reveal-change` | `Event` | When the user reveals or hides the swipe actions. |
| `toggle` | `Event` | When the user expands or collapses an `expandable` item (`ToggleEvent`). |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Used to display the item's primary label. |
| `avatar` | Used to display a circular avatar before the item's content. |
| `leading` | Used to display icons and content before the item's main content. |
| `leading-actions` | Buttons revealed by swiping toward the end. |
| `nested` | Nested items or content an `expandable` item reveals. |
| `overline` | Used to display overline text above the main label. |
| `supporting-text` | Used to display supporting text below the main label. |
| `trailing` | Used to display icons and content after the item's main content. |
| `trailing-actions` | Buttons revealed by swiping toward the start. The last one is the primary action. |
| `trailing-text` | Used to display metadata or text after the item's main content. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `actions` | The container of the revealed actions. |
| `drag-handle` | The reorder drag handle. |
| `list-item` | The list item's root element. |
| `move-down` | The button that moves a grabbed item down. |
| `move-up` | The button that moves a grabbed item up. |
| `nested` | The expandable region of nested content. |
| `reveal-button` | The "more" button that reveals the swipe actions. |

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `color` | `color` | `"standard" \| "vibrant"` | `MENU_COLORS.standard` | The color style: `standard` (surface-based) or `vibrant` (tertiary). |
| `contextFor` | `context-for` | `string` | `''` | The id of an element (in the same root) that opens this menu as a context menu on a secondary click, the context menu key or a long press (menus.md "Context menus ... usually open with a secondary click"; "Performs a specific action to trigger the menu, like right-click or press-and-hold"). |
| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density, from 0 (default) to -3 (menus.md "Density": "On web only, density levels control the spacing between elements. Increasing density decreases the top and bottom padding"). Each step removes 4px from the items' height; submenus inherit it. Values are clamped to that range. Dense items are smaller than the 48px recommended target, so don't apply density by default; let people opt in (grids-spacing.md "Avoid applying component scaling by default"). |
| `filterFor` | `filter-for` | `string` | `''` | The id of a text field (in the same root) the menu is attached to. As the person types in it, the menu opens (without taking focus) and shows only the items whose label contains the text, case-insensitively (menus.md "Filtering": "As someone types, the list of menu options filters to show relevant results"). The remaining items ease into their new position ("Menu items ease into their new position as the menu is filtered"), unless motion is reduced. Fires `filter` with the query. |
| `layout` | `layout` (reflects) | `"standard" \| "grouped"` | `'standard'` | The layout: `standard` items on one surface, or `grouped` `md-gb-menu-group`s on separate surfaces with gaps between them. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `closeAll` | `() => void` | Closes this menu and every menu it is a submenu of. |
| `filter` | `(query: string) => void` | Shows only the items whose label contains `query`, easing the others into their new position. |
| `showAt` | `(x: number, y: number) => void` | Opens the menu at a point in the viewport (a context menu), keeping it inside the window: it opens below and after the point, and flips when there is no room. |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | The menu's items, groups and dividers. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `menu` | The menu's root element. |

### `components/navdrawer/`

#### `<md-gb-nav-drawer>`

> **Deprecated.** "The navigation drawer is no longer recommended in the Material 3 Expressive update" (navigation-drawer.md). Use an expanded navigation rail (`<md-gb-nav-rail expanded>`), which has mostly the same functionality. The drawer stays for apps that haven't moved yet.

A Material Design navigation drawer custom element.

- Class: `NavDrawerElement` in `components/navdrawer/nav-drawer-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/navdrawer/md-gb-nav-drawer.js';`
- Spec: [navigation-drawer](https://m3.material.io/components/navigation-drawer) (Deprecated: no longer recommended in M3 Expressive. Use the expanded navigation rail.)
- Spec sections cited: `navigation-drawer/specs/measurements/standard-navigation-drawer`, `navigation-drawer/guidelines/usage`, `navigation-drawer/specs/measurements/modal-navigation-drawer`, `navigation-drawer/guidelines/anatomy`, `navigation-drawer/guidelines/usage/modal-navigation-drawer`, `navigation-drawer/specs`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `gesturesDisabled` | `gestures-disabled` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether swiping a modal drawer closed is turned off (Compose `gesturesEnabled = false`). Escape, the scrim and `close()` still close it. |
| `label` | `label` | `string` | `''` | The accessible name of the navigation landmark. Use it instead of `aria-label` when the host sits outside any landmark: the host's `aria-label` moves to a data attribute that checkers (axe `region`) still read as a name on a host that has no landmark role. |
| `modal` | `modal` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the drawer is modal: it opens over a scrim that blocks the rest of the app, instead of sitting next to the content. |
| `open` | `open` (reflects) | `boolean` |   | Whether the drawer is open. |
| `quick` | `quick` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether to skip the open and close transitions. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `(reason?: string) => Promise<void>` | Closes the drawer. Resolves once it has finished animating. |
| `show` | `() => Promise<void>` | Opens the drawer. Resolves once it has finished animating. |
| `toggle` | `() => Promise<void>` | Opens the drawer when closed, closes it when open. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `change` | `Event` | Fired when the user selects a different item. --bubbles |
| `close` | `CustomEvent<{reason: string}>` | Fired when the drawer starts closing. --cancelable |
| `closed` | `CustomEvent<{reason: string}>` | Fired when the drawer has closed and finished animating. |
| `open` | `Event` | Fired when the drawer starts opening. --cancelable |
| `opened` | `Event` | Fired when the drawer has opened and finished animating. |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Navigation items (`md-gb-nav-drawer-item`), section labels (headings or `.nav-drawer-section-label`) and dividers (`md-gb-divider` or `<hr>`). |
| `headline` | Optional headline shown above the items. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `dialog` | The modal drawer's `<dialog>`. |
| `drawer` | The drawer sheet. |
| `nav` | The `<nav>` landmark. |
| `scrim` | The modal drawer's scrim. |

### `components/navrail/`

#### `<md-gb-nav-rail>`

A Material Design navigation rail custom element.

- Class: `NavRailElement` in `components/navrail/nav-rail-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/navrail/md-gb-nav-rail.js';`
- Spec: [navigation-rail](https://m3.material.io/components/navigation-rail)
- Spec sections cited: `navigation-rail/specs/anatomy`, `navigation-rail/guidelines/anatomy`, `navigation-rail/specs/variants`, `navigation-rail/specs/baseline-navigation-rail`, `navigation-rail/specs/baseline-navigation-rail/configurations`, `navigation-rail/specs/variants/baseline-variants`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `alignment` | `alignment` (reflects) | `"center" \| "top" \| "bottom"` | `'top'` | Vertical alignment of items ('top', 'center', or 'bottom'). |
| `autoSelect` | `autoselect` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether or not to automatically select a tab when it is focused. |
| `collapsible` | `collapsible` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the navigation rail renders a built-in expand/collapse menu button in the header when no toggle button is slotted into `slot="header"`. |
| `divider` | `divider` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether a vertical divider marks the edge of the rail next to the app's content. |
| `elevated` | `elevated` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the rail is elevated to level 1, to distinguish it from content that scrolls underneath it. |
| `expanded` | `expanded` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the navigation rail is expanded to show horizontal items. |
| `hideWhenCollapsed` | `hide-when-collapsed` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Hide when collapsed (navigation-rail.md): the collapsed rail is not shown and takes no space, so the body content fills in. The expanded rail (always modal in this mode) appears over the content when the app's menu button calls `expand()` or `toggle()`; keep that menu button accessible. |
| `itemLayout` | `item-layout` (reflects) | `"horizontal" \| "vertical"` | `'vertical'` | The layout orientation of items ('vertical' or 'horizontal'). |
| `modal` | `modal` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the expanded navigation rail is modal: it opens over the content with a scrim, with modal elevation and shape. |
| `narrow` | `narrow` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the collapsed rail uses the narrow width (`md.comp.nav-rail.collapsed.narrow.container.width`, 80dp). |
| `orientation` | `orientation` (reflects) | `"horizontal" \| "vertical"` | `'vertical'` | Orientation of the tablist ('horizontal' or 'vertical'). |
| `selectedIndex` | `selected-index` | `number` |   | The 0-based index of the currently active navigation rail item. Alias for `selectedTabIndex`. |
| `selectedTabIndex` | `selectedtabindex` | `number` |   | The index of the currently selected tab. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `collapse` | `() => void` | Collapses the navigation rail. |
| `expand` | `() => void` | Expands the navigation rail. |
| `handleBack` | `() => Promise<void>` | Handles a back request (the Android back gesture or button) on the modal expanded rail: plays the predictive back motion, then collapses the rail. Call it for an in-app back control that should behave like the system back. Resolves after the back motion. |
| `toggle` | `(force?: boolean) => boolean` | Toggles the expanded state of the navigation rail. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `change` | `Event` | Fired when the selected item changes. --bubbles |
| `toggle` | `CustomEvent<{expanded: boolean, reason?: string}>` | Fired when the expanded state is toggled. --bubbles |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Used to display navigation rail items (`md-gb-nav-rail-item`). |
| `header` | Used to display optional header controls (menu button, FAB). |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `header` | The navigation rail's header container. |
| `items` | The navigation rail's items container. |
| `menu-button` | The built-in expand/collapse menu button when `collapsible`. |
| `nav-rail` | The navigation rail's root container. |
| `scrim` | The modal rail's scrim, while expanded. A `modal` rail expands over the content with a scrim, like Compose `ModalWideNavigationRail`. Clicking the scrim, pressing Escape or swiping the rail toward its edge collapses it. The scrim (and the menu button of a `collapsible` rail) are the single-pointer alternatives to the swipe. While a modal rail is expanded, focus moves into it, Tab stays inside it, page scrolling is locked, and focus returns to the previous element when it collapses. The `toggle` event's `detail.reason` says why a modal rail collapsed (`escape`, `back`, `scrim` or `swipe`). Predictive back applies to the modal expanded rail (navigation-rail.md): while it is expanded the rail listens for back requests with a `CloseWatcher` (the Android back gesture or button, and Escape), falling back to Escape on the document where `CloseWatcher` is missing. A back request plays the predictive back motion (the rail pops off its edge, Compose `ModalWideNavigationRail` scales) and collapses the rail. A FAB (`md-gb-fab` with a `label`) in the header is an extended FAB while the rail is expanded and an icon FAB while it is collapsed. |

### `components/radio/`

#### `<md-gb-radio>`

A Material Design component.

- Class: `RadioElement` in `components/radio/radio-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/radio/md-gb-radio.js';`
- Spec: [radio-button](https://m3.material.io/components/radio-button)
- Spec sections cited: `radio-button/specs`, `radio-button/guidelines/anatomy`, `radio-button/guidelines/usage`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `checked` | `checked` | `boolean` |   | Whether or not the radio is selected. |
| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density, from 0 (default) to -3. Each step down removes 4px from the radio's layout box (48px) and state layer (40px); the touch target stays 48px. Values are clamped to that range. radio-button.md: don't apply density by default; let people opt in to a denser layout. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | `boolean` |   | Disabled by the attribute or by a disabled fieldset. |
| `name` | `name` | `string` |   | The HTML name to use in form submission. |
| `required` | `required` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether or not the radio is required. If any radio is required in a group, all radios are implicitly required. |
| `value` | `value` | `string` | `'on'` | The element value to use in form submission when checked. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `formDisabledCallback` | `(disabled: boolean) => void` | A callback for when a form component should be disabled or enabled. This can be called in a variety of situations, such as disabled `<fieldset>`s. |
| `formResetCallback` | `() => void` | An optional callback for when the form requests to reset its value. Typically, the default value that is reset is represented in the attribute of an element. This means the attribute used for the value should not update as the value changes. For example, a checkbox should not change its default `checked` attribute when selected. Ensure form values do not reflect. |
| `formStateRestoreCallback` | `(state: string) => void` | An optional callback for when the form restores the state of a component. For example, when a page is reloaded or forms are autofilled. |

### `components/sheet/`

#### `<md-gb-bottom-sheet>`

A Material Design bottom sheet.

- Class: `BottomSheetElement` in `components/sheet/bottom-sheet-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/sheet/md-gb-bottom-sheet.js';`
- Spec: [bottom-sheets](https://m3.material.io/components/bottom-sheets), [side-sheets](https://m3.material.io/components/side-sheets)

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `closeLabel` | `close-label` | `string` | `'Close'` | Accessible label of the close icon button of a full-screen sheet. |
| `collapseLabel` | `collapse-label` | `string` | `'Collapse'` | Accessible label of the collapse icon button of a full-screen sheet. |
| `dragHandleLabel` | `drag-handle-label` | `string` | `'Drag handle'` | Accessible label of the drag handle button. |
| `hideDragHandle` | `hide-drag-handle` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether to hide the drag handle (which also disables dragging). |
| `label` | `label` | `string` | `'Bottom sheet'` | Accessible label of the sheet dialog. |
| `modal` | `modal` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the sheet is modal: shown above a scrim, blocking the rest of the page, and closed by Escape or a scrim click. |
| `open` | `open` (reflects) | `boolean` |   | Whether the sheet is open. Setting it calls `show()` or `close()`. |
| `quick` | `quick` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether to skip the opening and closing motion. |
| `skipPartiallyExpanded` | `skip-partially-expanded` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the sheet has no partially expanded height: it opens expanded and the drag handle closes it. |
| `state` | `state` (reflects) | `"expanded" \| "partially-expanded"` | `BOTTOM_SHEET_STATES.partiallyExpanded` | The resting height while open: `partially-expanded` or `expanded`. Updated when the user drags or activates the drag handle. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `(reason?: string) => Promise<void>` | Closes the sheet. Resolves after the closing motion. |
| `expand` | `() => void` | Expands the sheet to its full height. |
| `handleBack` | `() => Promise<void>` | Handles a back request (the Android back gesture or button, which a modal sheet receives through its dialog): plays the predictive back motion and closes the sheet with reason `back`. If the `close` event is canceled, the sheet settles back. Resolves after the closing motion. Call it for an in-app back control that should behave like the system back. |
| `partialExpand` | `() => void` | Moves the sheet to its partially expanded height, when it has one. |
| `render` | `() => TemplateResult<1>` | Invoked on each update to perform rendering tasks. This method may return any value renderable by lit-html's `ChildPart` - typically a `TemplateResult`. Setting properties inside this method will *not* trigger the element to update. |
| `show` | `() => Promise<void>` | Opens the sheet. Resolves after the opening motion. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `CustomEvent<{reason: string}>` | Fired when the sheet begins closing. Cancelable; canceling keeps it open. |
| `closed` | `CustomEvent<{reason: string}>` | Fired after the closing motion. |
| `open` | `Event` | Fired when the sheet begins opening. Cancelable. |
| `opened` | `Event` | Fired after the opening motion. |
| `state-change` | `Event` | Fired when the user moves the sheet to another height (`state`). |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | The sheet content. It scrolls when taller than the sheet. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `container` | The sheet container. |
| `content` | The container of the default slot. |
| `dialog` | The `<dialog>` holding the sheet; its `::backdrop` is the scrim. |
| `drag-handle` | The drag handle button. |

### `components/snackbar/`

#### `<md-gb-snackbar>`

A Material Design snackbar component.

- Class: `SnackbarElement` in `components/snackbar/snackbar-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/snackbar/md-gb-snackbar.js';`
- Spec: [snackbar](https://m3.material.io/components/snackbar)
- Spec sections cited: `snackbar/guidelines/anatomy`, `snackbar/specs`, `snackbar/specs/configurations`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `actionLabel` | `action-label` | `string` | `''` | Visible text of the built-in action button. Rendered only when non-empty and nothing is slotted into `action`. |
| `align` | `align` (reflects) | `"start" \| "center"` | `'center'` | Where a snackbar sits on wide layouts: `center` (default) or `start` (left-aligned). Use the same value wherever snackbars appear, so they always show in the same spot. Compact layouts span the width. |
| `anchor` | `anchor` | `string` | `''` | The id of an element the snackbar appears above, such as a FAB or a docked toolbar, in the snackbar's root (document or shadow root). See `anchorElement`. |
| `anchorElement` |   | `HTMLElement` | `null` | An element the snackbar appears above, such as a FAB or a docked toolbar: while open, the snackbar's bottom edge sits `--bottom-offset` above the element's top edge. Takes precedence over `anchor`. |
| `closeLabel` | `close-label` | `string` | `'Close'` | Accessible label for the built-in dismiss button. |
| `fixed` | `fixed` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the snackbar should be positioned relative to the viewport (fixed). |
| `hasCloseButton` | `has-close-button` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether to display a close icon button. |
| `labelText` | `label-text` | `string` | `''` | Supporting message text to display. Rendered before default slot content. |
| `open` | `open` (reflects) | `boolean` |   | Whether the snackbar is currently open. |
| `quick` | `quick` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether to skip opening and closing transition delays. |
| `timeoutMs` | `timeout-ms` | `number` | `5000` | Time in milliseconds before the snackbar automatically dismisses. Set to 0 or a negative value (e.g. -1) to disable auto-dismiss. Read when the snackbar opens; changing it while open takes effect on the next open. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `(reason?: string) => Promise<void>` | Closes the snackbar. |
| `render` | `() => TemplateResult<1>` | Invoked on each update to perform rendering tasks. This method may return any value renderable by lit-html's `ChildPart` - typically a `TemplateResult`. Setting properties inside this method will *not* trigger the element to update. |
| `show` | `() => Promise<void>` | Opens the snackbar. |
| `updateAnchorPosition` | `() => void` | Places the snackbar above its anchor. Called while open when the window or the anchor resizes; call it after moving the anchor. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `CustomEvent<{reason: string}>` | Fired when the snackbar begins closing. |
| `closed` | `CustomEvent<{reason: string}>` | Fired when the snackbar has closed. |
| `open` | `Event` | Fired when the snackbar begins opening. |
| `opened` | `Event` | Fired when the snackbar has opened and animations finished. |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Default slot for rich content (rendered after `label-text`). If both `label-text` and slotted content are provided, both render. |
| `action` | Slot for a custom action button; replaces the built-in action rendered from `action-label`. Prefer the `action-label` attribute, which renders a text button matching the spec. A slotted plain `<button>` is styled by the snackbar, with hover, focus and pressed states. A slotted text `<md-gb-button>` gets the `--action-label-text*` colors and typography, but keeps its own 16px padding and pressed shape, because its component tokens can't be set from outside its shadow root. |
| `dismiss` | Slot for dismiss button (falls back to close icon button when `has-close-button` is set). |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `actions` | The container wrapping action and dismiss slots. |
| `label` | The container wrapping the label content. |
| `surface` | The surface container of the snackbar. |

This means the attribute used for the value should not update as the value changes. For example, a checkbox should not change its default `checked` attribute when selected. Ensure form values do not reflect. |

#### `<md-gb-tabs>`

A Material Design tabs component.

- Class: `TabsElement` in `components/tabs/tabs-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/components/tabs/md-gb-tabs.js';`
- Spec: [tabs](https://m3.material.io/components/tabs)

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `align` | `align` (reflects) | `"start" \| "center"` | `'center'` | Where fixed tabs sit when the window is wider than compact: `center` (default) or `start`, the leading edge. tabs.md, Responsive layout: "The group of tabs should use a fluid margin and align to the center or leading edge of the body region." At compact windows the tabs fill the full width. |
| `autoSelect` | `autoselect` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether or not to automatically select a tab when it is focused. |
| `density` | `density` (reflects) | `0 \| -1 \| -2 \| -3` |   | The density, from 0 (default) to -3. Each step down removes 4px from the tabs' height (48px, or 64px with icon and label); values are clamped to that range. tabs.md "Avoid applying density by default": dense tabs are below the 48x48 CSS pixel target, so let people opt in (a denser layout or theme) and keep the controls that change it at 48x48. |
| `orientation` | `orientation` (reflects) | `"horizontal" \| "vertical"` | `'horizontal'` | Orientation of the tablist ('horizontal' or 'vertical'). |
| `scrollable` | `scrollable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Scrollable tabs: each tab's width is defined by its label (tabs.md) rather than every tab taking the width of the widest, and the bar scrolls when they overflow. Fixed tabs (the default) are equal. |
| `scrollBehavior` | `scroll-behavior` (reflects) | `"hide"` |   | How the tabs react to scrolling: `hide` scrolls them off the screen when the page scrolls down and returns them when it scrolls up (tabs.md). Tabs attached to an app bar (its `tabs` slot) leave this unset: the app bar moves them with itself as one unit. |
| `scrollTarget` | `scroll-target` | `string` | `''` | The id of the element whose scrolling drives `scroll-behavior`. Defaults to the window. |
| `selectedTabIndex` | `selectedtabindex` | `number` |   | The index of the currently selected tab. |
| `variant` | `variant` (reflects) | `"primary" \| "secondary"` | `'primary'` | The visual variant of the tabs. |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | Used to display `<md-gb-tab>` children. |

Each step down removes 4px from the time selectors (80px), input fields (72px), the period selector (which follows their height) and the dial (256px); the time selectors, fields and dial labels stay at least 48px. The horizontal layout, used when the viewport is short, keeps the full-size dial ("Don't apply density to the time picker dial when the viewport is constrained. Instead, use an input picker"). Values are clamped to that range. |
| `dialModeLabel` | `dial-mode-label` | `string` | `'Switch to clock mode'` | Accessible label of the mode toggle in input mode. |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | `boolean` |   | Disabled by the attribute or by a disabled fieldset. |
| `headline` | `headline` | `string` | `''` | The headline. Defaults to "Select time" (dial) or "Enter time" (input). |
| `hideActions` | `hide-actions` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Hides the Cancel and OK actions; every change commits the value. |
| `hideModeToggle` | `hide-mode-toggle` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Hides the dial / input mode toggle button. |
| `hourCycle` | `hour-cycle` | `"" \| "12" \| "24"` | `''` | `12` (with an AM/PM period selector), `24`, or `''` (default) to follow the locale's clock (`Intl.DateTimeFormat`). |
| `hourLabel` | `hour-label` | `string` | `'Hour'` | Hour field label (input mode). |
| `inputModeLabel` | `input-mode-label` | `string` | `'Switch to text input mode'` | Accessible label of the mode toggle in dial mode. |
| `locale` | `locale` | `string` | `''` | The locale used for the 12/24-hour default and the AM/PM labels. Defaults to the browser locale. |
| `minuteLabel` | `minute-label` | `string` | `'Minute'` | Minute field label (input mode). |
| `modal` | `modal` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Renders the picker as a modal dialog, shown with `show()` or `open`. |
| `mode` | `mode` (reflects) | `"input" \| "dial"` | `'dial'` | `dial` (clock dial) or `input` (hour and minute text fields). |
| `name` | `name` | `string` |   | The HTML name to use in form submission. |
| `open` | `open` (reflects) | `boolean` |   | Whether a modal picker is open. Setting it opens or closes the picker. |
| `orientation` | `orientation` (reflects) | `"" \| "horizontal" \| "vertical"` | `''` | The dial layout: `vertical`, `horizontal`, or `''` (default) to pick one from the viewport (horizontal in short landscape viewports). |
| `periodLabel` | `period-label` | `string` | `'Select AM or PM'` | Accessible label of the period selector. |
| `quick` | `quick` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Skips transitions. |
| `required` | `required` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether a time is required: with no time the picker is invalid (`valueMissing`) and blocks the form's submit. |
| `selectHourLabel` | `select-hour-label` | `string` | `'Select hour'` | Accessible label of the hour selector and the dial in hour mode. |
| `selection` |   | `"hour" \| "minute"` | `'hour'` | Which part of the time the dial edits. |
| `selectMinuteLabel` | `select-minute-label` | `string` | `'Select minutes'` | Accessible label of the minute selector and the dial in minute mode. |
| `value` | `value` | `string` |   | The committed time as a 24-hour `HH:MM` string (`<input type="time">` format), or `''` when no time is set. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `cancel` | `(reason?: string) => void` | Discards the pending time and fires `cancel`; a modal picker closes unless the event is canceled (canceling keeps the pending time). What the Cancel action does. |
| `close` | `(reason?: string) => Promise<void>` | Closes a modal picker without committing. Fires a cancelable `close` event with `detail.reason`, then `closed` after the exit transition. |
| `confirm` | `() => void` | Commits the pending time to `value` (firing `change` when it differs) and closes a modal picker. What the OK action does. |
| `formDisabledCallback` | `(disabled: boolean) => void` | A callback for when a form component should be disabled or enabled. This can be called in a variety of situations, such as disabled `<fieldset>`s. |
| `formResetCallback` | `() => void` | An optional callback for when the form requests to reset its value. Typically, the default value that is reset is represented in the attribute of an element. This means the attribute used for the value should not update as the value changes. For example, a checkbox should not change its default `checked` attribute when selected. Ensure form values do not reflect. |
| `formStateRestoreCallback` | `(state: string) => void` | An optional callback for when the form restores the state of a component. For example, when a page is reloaded or forms are autofilled. |
| `show` | `() => Promise<void>` | Opens a modal picker on the committed time, starting with the hours. Fires `open`. |
| `toggleMode` | `() => void` | Switches between the dial and input modes. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `cancel` | `Event` | Fired when picking is canceled (Cancel, Escape, scrim click). Cancelable for a modal picker; canceling keeps it open. |
| `change` | `Event` | Fired when a new `value` is committed (OK, or the end of an interaction with `hide-actions`). |
| `close` | `Event` | Fired when a modal picker closes. |
| `input` | `Event` | Fired whenever the picked time changes (dial drag, key, typed digits, period). Read the pending time from `pendingValue`. |
| `open` | `Event` | Fired when a modal picker opens. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `actions` | The actions row. |
| `container` | The picker container (a `<dialog>` when modal). |
| `dial` | The clock dial. |
| `headline` | The headline. |

| `expanded` | `expanded` (reflects) | `boolean` | `true` | Whether a floating toolbar with a FAB shows its items. When `false`, the toolbar collapses and its FAB grows to the medium size (Compose floating toolbar `expanded`). Set `expanded="false"` from markup. |

| `trailing` | Used to display trailing toolbar items. |

| `align` | `align` (reflects) | `"" \| "start" \| "center"` | `''` | How the tooltip lines up with its anchor along the placement side: `center` (plain default) or `start` (rich default). A rich tooltip can be `center`ed below its anchor on desktop. |
| `caret` | `caret` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether to draw a caret pointing at the anchor. |
| `hideDelay` | `hide-delay` | `number` | `TOOLTIP_HIDE_DELAY_MS` | Delay before hiding after the pointer leaves, in milliseconds. |

| `open` | `open` (reflects) | `boolean` |   | Whether the tooltip is open. |
| `persistent` | `persistent` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Rich only: opens when the anchor is clicked instead of on hover / focus, and stays open until the user interacts with another element. |
| `placement` | `placement` (reflects) | `"" \| "start" \| "end" \| "above" \| "below"` | `''` | Preferred side of the anchor. Defaults to `above` for plain tooltips and `below` for rich ones. Flips when there isn't room. |
| `quick` | `quick` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Skips the open / close animation. |
| `showDelay` | `show-delay` | `number` | `TOOLTIP_SHOW_DELAY_MS` | Hover delay before showing, in milliseconds. |
| `subhead` | `subhead` | `string` | `''` | Rich only: subhead text. Replaced by content in the `subhead` slot. |
| `variant` | `variant` (reflects) | `"plain" \| "rich"` | `TOOLTIP_VARIANTS.plain` | Plain or rich. |

## Extras

The library's own components, with no page of their own in the M3 components index.

### `extras/demo/`

#### `<md-gb-demo>`

An example and its code.

- Class: `DemoElement` in `extras/demo/demo-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/extras/demo/md-gb-demo.js';`
- Spec: none (No M3 page: an example and its code, as documentation shows them.)

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `expanded` | `expanded` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether long code shows in full. |
| `label` | `label` | `string` | `''` | The accessible name of the demo (what it shows). |
| `previewHeight` | `preview-height` | `number` | `0` | The block size the preview reserves (px), so the page doesn't move as it loads. |
| `sandbox` | `sandbox` | `string` | `'allow-scripts allow-forms'` | The `sandbox` of the `srcdoc` frame. By default scripts and forms run in an opaque origin, so the document can't reach the page, and it can only load cross-origin resources that send CORS headers. Add `allow-same-origin` only for documents you trust, such as your own examples: they then share the page's origin, cache and fonts. |
| `srcdoc` | `srcdoc` | `string` | `''` | A document to show as the example instead of the slotted elements (an example with its own script or styles): it renders in a sandboxed iframe, sized to the height it posts. |

| `fullscreen` | `fullscreen` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the scaffold is the app's root layout and fills the window (the viewport, past the body's margins), so a page needs no styles of its own to lay out its bars, rails and content. |

A side sheet is present while the nested pane scaffold shows its tertiary pane or `side-sheet-open` is set. |
