#### `<md-gb-pane-scaffold>`

A Material Design pane scaffold (list-detail, supporting pane, feed).

- Class: `PaneScaffoldElement` in `extras/scaffold/pane-scaffold-element.js`
- Import: `import '@material/web/extras/scaffold/md-gb-pane-scaffold.js';`
- Spec: [scaffold](https://m3.material.io/foundations/layout/scaffold) (A Foundations > Layout page, not a component.)
- Spec sections cited: `layout-canonical-examples/list-detail/usage/dividing-space`, `layout-canonical-examples/list-detail/across-breakpoints/medium`, `layout-canonical-examples/supporting-pane/dividing-space`, `layout-canonical-examples/feed/across-breakpoints/compact`, `layout-canonical-examples/list-detail`, `layout-canonical-examples/list-detail/across-breakpoints/expanded-large-extra-large`, `layout-canonical-examples/list-detail/across-breakpoints/compact`, `layout-bidirectionality-rtl/tab-1/canonical-layout-examples/list-detail`, `layout-scaffold/panes/panes`, `layout-scaffold/panes/panes/two-pane-layouts`

| Property | Attribute | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `arrangement` | `arrangement` (reflects) | `"fixed-flexible" \| "split"` | `'fixed-flexible'` | How two panes share the width. |
| `collapsedPane` | `collapsed-pane` (reflects) | `"primary" \| "secondary"` | `null` | A pane collapsed with the drag handle, or null. |
| `detailOpen` | `detail-open` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | List-detail: whether an item is selected. Single-pane windows show the detail (primary) pane when true and the list (secondary) pane when false; two-pane windows show the `placeholder` slot instead of the detail while false. |
| `dragHandleLabel` | `drag-handle-label` | `string` | `'Resize panes'` | Accessible label of the drag handle. |
| `handleTap` | `handle-tap` | `"toggle" \| "cycle"` | `'toggle'` | What a tap, Enter or Space on the drag handle does, the single-pointer alternative to dragging it: `toggle` (default) switches between one and two panes; `cycle` steps through the snap widths and then collapses the secondary pane. |
| `layout` | `layout` (reflects) | `"list-detail" \| "supporting-pane" \| "feed"` | `'list-detail'` | The canonical layout. |
| `levitate` | `levitate` (reflects) | `"none" \| "docked" \| "floating"` | `'none'` | The levitate strategy for the secondary pane of supporting pane and feed layouts in windows too small for two panes: `none` reflows it below the primary pane, `floating` floats it above, `docked` docks it to the bottom edge at compact (floating from medium). |
| `levitateCoplanar` | `levitate-coplanar` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Keeps a levitated pane co-planar at large and extra-large windows. By default it floats there (spec: scaffold, "On large screens: floating panes are the default"). |
| `levitateDraggable` | `levitate-draggable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Lets a floating pane be dragged by its handle, or moved with the arrow keys while the handle is focused (spec: scaffold, floating panes "can be customized to be dragged or resized"). |
| `levitateLabel` | `levitate-label` | `string` | `''` | Accessible label of a modal levitated pane (its dialog role). |
| `levitateModal` | `levitate-modal` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the levitated secondary pane is modal (scrim, inert panes). |
| `levitateMoveLabel` | `levitate-move-label` | `string` | `'Move pane'` | Accessible label of the floating pane's move handle. |
| `levitateOpen` | `levitate-open` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether the levitated secondary pane is shown. |
| `levitateResizable` | `levitate-resizable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Lets a floating pane be resized by its corner handle, or with the arrow keys while the handle is focused. |
| `levitateResizeLabel` | `levitate-resize-label` | `string` | `'Resize pane'` | Accessible label of the floating pane's resize handle. |
| `mediumTwoPane` | `medium-two-pane` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether medium windows show two panes (50% each) instead of one. The spec limits this to low-density content such as settings. |
| `paneWidth` | `pane-width` | `number` | `null` | Width in px of the secondary pane set with the drag handle, or null for the default width. Set it to restore a persisted width. |
| `resizable` | `resizable` (reflects) | `boolean` | `false` | Whether two-pane layouts render a drag handle to resize the panes. |
| `resizeBehavior` | `resize-behavior` | `"persistent" \| "temporary"` | `undefined` | `persistent` keeps a resized width or collapsed pane across window size class changes; `temporary` returns to the default layout. Defaults to persistent for list-detail and temporary for the other layouts. |
| `scrollLabel` | `scroll-label` | `string` | `'Panes'` | Accessible label of the stacked panes' scroll region. Only used while the stacked panes overflow and hold nothing focusable: the region is then focusable (so the keyboard can scroll it) and a labelled `region`. |
| `secondaryPosition` | `secondary-position` (reflects) | `"start" \| "end"` | `undefined` | Where the secondary pane sits in two-pane layouts. Defaults to `start` for list-detail and `end` for supporting pane and feed layouts. Mirrors in right-to-left. |

| Method | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `closeDetail` | `() => void` | List-detail: returns to the list (e.g. from the detail pane's back button in a single-pane window). |
| `closeLevitated` | `() => void` | Hides the levitated secondary pane. |
| `measureFold` | `() => void` | Finds where the viewport's fold crosses the scaffold. Call it after moving the scaffold without resizing it. |
| `openDetail` | `() => void` | List-detail: shows the detail pane (e.g. after selecting an item). |
| `openLevitated` | `() => void` | Shows the levitated secondary pane. |
| `resetPaneWidth` | `() => void` | Returns the panes to their default widths. |

| Event | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `levitate-close` | `Event` | Fired when the scaffold closes a levitated pane (Escape, or using something behind a modal pane). --bubbles --composed |
| `pane-layout-change` | `CustomEvent<PaneLayoutChangeDetail>` | Fired when the visible panes change. --bubbles --composed |
| `pane-resize` | `CustomEvent<PaneResizeDetail>` | Fired when the panes are resized or collapsed with the drag handle. --bubbles --composed |
| `window-class-change` | `CustomEvent<WindowClassChangeDetail>` | Fired when the window size class changes. --bubbles --composed |
| `window-height-class-change` | `CustomEvent<WindowHeightClassChangeDetail>` | Fired when the window height size class changes. --bubbles --composed |

| Slot | Description |
| --- | --- |
| (default) | The primary (flexible) pane: the detail pane of a list-detail layout, the focus pane of a supporting pane layout, or the feed items (laid out on a grid) of a feed layout. |
| `placeholder` | Shown in the primary pane of a two-pane list-detail layout while `detail-open` is false (no item selected). Levitate (spec: scaffold, "Levitate"): with `levitate="floating"` or `levitate="docked"`, the secondary pane of a supporting pane or feed layout floats above the primary pane (or docks to the bottom edge at compact, like a bottom sheet) in windows too small for two co-planar panes, while `levitate-open` is set. `levitate-modal` makes it modal: a scrim covers the panes, the primary pane is inert, focus moves into the pane and back to the trigger, and the pane closes when something behind it is used or Escape is pressed. Modality is scoped to the scaffold; use `md-gb-dialog` or `md-gb-sheet` for app-wide modals. At large and extra-large windows a levitated pane keeps floating (spec: "On large screens, floating panes are the default"); the scrim is optional (`levitate-modal` adds it). `levitate-coplanar` opts into a co-planar pane there instead. `levitate-draggable` and `levitate-resizable` add a move handle and a resize corner to a floating pane: drag them, or focus them and use the arrow keys (Shift moves in larger steps). `levitate-change` fires after each move or resize. Foldables: the scaffold reads the fold (hinge) from the Viewport Segments API and keeps panes off it. A vertical fold splits two panes at the fold with the spacer on it (at least the spacer width); a horizontal fold (tabletop) stacks the supporting pane below it. The fold crossing the scaffold is reflected as the `fold` attribute (`vertical` or `horizontal`). Browsers without the API keep the regular layout. |
| `secondary` | The secondary pane: the list of a list-detail layout or the supporting pane. It is the fixed pane in fixed-and-flexible arrangements. |
| `tertiary` | An optional third pane (e.g. a standard side sheet), shown at extra-large only. |

| CSS part | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `drag-handle` | The drag handle. |
| `pane` | Each pane. |
| `primary` | The primary pane. |
| `scaffold` | The flex container of the panes. |
| `scrim` | The scrim behind a modal levitated pane. |
| `secondary` | The secondary pane. |
| `spacer` | The spacer between panes. |
| `tertiary` | The tertiary pane. |

</details>

A tabindex the app set is left as it is. It follows the element's size and its content's (a ResizeObserver on both). Returns a function that stops it. |

The click that follows a drag is suppressed, so a drag over a button or link does not activate it. |

#### `foundations/interaction/gestures/predictive-back.js`

- Spec: [gestures](https://m3.material.io/foundations/interaction/gestures)

| Export | Kind | Signature | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `BACK_REASON` | variable | `"back"` | Close reason for a back request (Android back gesture or button). |
| `BackHandler` | class | `class BackHandler` | Listens for back requests while a non-dialog surface is open, like Compose `BackHandler`. Uses a `CloseWatcher` where supported (Escape, and the Android back gesture or button); elsewhere it falls back to an Escape listener on the document. Handlers that already handle Escape should call `preventDefault()`, which stops both the close watcher and the fallback. |
| `BOTTOM_SHEET_PREDICTIVE_BACK_MAX_X_PX` | variable | `48` | Compose `BottomSheet.kt` `PredictiveBackMaxScaleXDistance` (48dp) and `PredictiveBackMaxScaleYDistance` (24dp): how far the sheet shrinks. |
| `BOTTOM_SHEET_PREDICTIVE_BACK_MAX_Y_PX` | variable | `24` | See `BOTTOM_SHEET_PREDICTIVE_BACK_MAX_X_PX`. |
| `closeRequestSource` | function | `(event: Event): CloseRequestSource` | Where a dialog `cancel` event came from. The browser fires `cancel` for Escape and for the Android back gesture or button; a trusted `cancel` with no Escape press just before it is a back request. Synthetic `cancel` events count as Escape, as before predictive back. |
| `CloseRequestSource` | type | `typeof BACK_REASON \| typeof ESCAPE_REASON` | Where a close request came from. |
| `ESCAPE_REASON` | variable | `"escape"` | Close reason for the Escape key. |
| `playPredictiveBack` | function | `(surface: HTMLElement, options: PredictiveBackOptions): PredictiveBackAnimation` | Plays the predictive back transform on a surface. Returns null when motion is reduced or the element can't animate. |
| `PREDICTIVE_BACK_DURATION_MS` | variable | `200` | How long the back progress plays on close: `md.sys.motion.duration.short4`. Compose follows the finger instead; the web has no progress events, so this stands in for a quick back swipe. |
| `PREDICTIVE_BACK_EASING` | variable | `"cubic-bezier(0.1, 0.1, 0, 1)"` | Compose `PredictiveBackEasing` (material3 `internal/BackHandler.kt`), applied to the back progress. |
| `PredictiveBackAnimation` | class | `class PredictiveBackAnimation` | A running predictive back animation. It holds its end state (fill `forwards`) until cancelled or reversed. |
| `PredictiveBackKind` | type | `'bottom-sheet' \| 'side' \| 'search'` | The predictive back shape of a surface. |
| `PredictiveBackOptions` | interface | `{kind; measure; content; edge; duration}` | Options of `playPredictiveBack()`. |
| `predictiveBackTransform` | function | `(surface: HTMLElement, options: PredictiveBackOptions): { surface: { scale: string; transformOrigin: string; }; content?: { scale: string; transformOrigin: string; }; }` | The predictive back transform at full gesture progress, as CSS `scale` and `transform-origin` values for the surface and its content. |
| `prefersReducedMotion` | function | `(): boolean` | Whether motion should be skipped for reduced motion. |
| `resetEscapeTracking` | function | `(): void` | Forgets recorded Escape presses (for tests). |
| `SEARCH_PREDICTIVE_BACK_MIN_SCALE` | variable | `0.9` | Compose `SearchBar.kt` `SearchBarPredictiveBackMinScale` (9/10). |
| `SIDE_PREDICTIVE_BACK_MAX_X_PX` | variable | `24` | Compose `WideNavigationRail.kt` `PredictiveBackMaxScaleXDistance` (24dp) and `PredictiveBackMaxScaleYDistance` (48dp), used for edge surfaces (the modal rail and side sheets). |
| `SIDE_PREDICTIVE_BACK_MAX_Y_PX` | variable | `48` | See `SIDE_PREDICTIVE_BACK_MAX_X_PX`. |
| `trackEscapeKey` | function | `(): void` | Records trusted Escape presses, so close requests can tell Escape from the back gesture. Installed once, in the capture phase, before any component handler can stop the event. |
